# 前缀和与差分讲解图

四个区域：一维前缀和、一维差分、二维前缀和、二维差分。所有文字、数字、网格与箭头均可编辑。

配套讲义：[[前缀和与差分讲义]]

# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements

前缀和与差分 · 一维 / 二维 ^99357cfda58b43f8826e

统一使用 1 下标与闭区间：前缀和快速查询，差分记录批量更新的边界。 ^891a1fc5b5e14a0eacf3

定义：D[i] = a[i] − a[i−1]，a[0] = 0 ^23ebb1124502443f8801

[l,r] 加 v：D[l] += v；D[r+1] −= v ^f45f08d795844f3cac85

区间内部相邻两项都加 v，差值抵消；只留下两个边界。 ^18acbe69c9584a2e8cb3

单次更新 O(1)；还原 O(n)；预留 D[n+1] ^143ccdbfd1af48398562

累加还原 ^92cd760c02e94f7b9403

浅绿阴影标出目标范围；D 中的数是边界变化。 ^8f6c004858ff4e06a229

D[2][2] += 2                         D[4][2] −= 2 ^400dd50dba0641e79eb9

D[2][4] −= 2                         D[4][4] += 2 ^9784ac3db22c4519b30a

右边界与下边界的撤销位置都要 +1。 ^d5395fa7741d4f7298ab

四个标记怎样传播？每个标记影响自己的右下方。 ^f63e2fce4d1b45c6913d

① 开始：+2 ^1e3f55195d13464db367

+2 ^8b16fbf81bc848c7bb1b

② 撤销下方：−2 ^abb353d606d540ab8d98

−2 ^bd853b4949b94d00bf2d

③ 撤销右方：−2 ^1ae49b3e90854ac1ba63

−2 ^770ad932119c4d3ca1fb

−2 ^ef6dd141643f4fdc913d

−2 ^a830755525784bb58236

④ 补回重叠：+2 ^49773564323841bfbb31

+2 ^84f42a85f0fe40fa9eec

右下角的四份影响：+2 − 2 − 2 + 2 = 0，目标外恢复为 0。 ^710770b78052432cbc05

矩形加 v：左上 +v，左下外侧 −v，右上外侧 −v，右下外侧 +v ^c9e21bed3f6f4ae8a0fe

还原：A[i][j] = D[i][j] + A[i−1][j] + A[i][j−1] − A[i−1][j−1] ^b2bb0ef1c4a745bb8f5f

单次更新 O(1)；最后统一还原 O(nm)；总时间 O(nm+k) ^48889879ae774ac8a5a7

边界空间：保留第 n+1 行与第 m+1 列；初始非零时先构建差分。 ^551aa4a136b7456db021

记忆线索：前缀和减去多余部分；差分在开始处加入，在结束后的边界撤销。 ^40f83821b3bb4ee99c88

      "text": "定义：D[i] = a[i] − a[i−1]，a[0] = 0",
      "originalText": "定义：D[i] = a[i] − a[i−1]，a[0] = 0",
      "fontSize": 27,
      "fontFamily": 2,
      "textAlign": "left",
      "verticalAlign": "top",
      "containerId": null,
      "lineHeight": 1.35,

    },
    {
      "id": "f45f08d795844f3cac85",
      "type": "text",
      "x": 1640.0,
      "y": 1307.0,
      "width": 443.0,
      "height": 37.800000000000004,
      "angle": 0,
      "strokeColor": "#c2410c",
      "backgroundColor": "transparent",
      "fillStyle": "solid",
      "strokeWidth": 1,
      "strokeStyle": "solid",
      "roughness": 0,

      "roundness": null,
      "seed": 1,
      "version": 1,

      "isDeleted": false,
      "boundElements": null,
      "updated": 1791527507119,
      "link": null,
      "locked": false,
      "text": "[l,r] 加 v：D[l] += v；D[r+1] −= v",
      "originalText": "[l,r] 加 v：D[l] += v；D[r+1] −= v",
      "fontSize": 28,
      "fontFamily": 2,
      "textAlign": "left",
      "verticalAlign": "top",
      "containerId": null,
      "lineHeight": 1.35,
      "autoResize": true,
      "baseline": 29.400000000000002
    },
    {
      "id": "18acbe69c9584a2e8cb3",
      "type": "text",
      "x": 1640.0,
      "y": 1349.0,
      "width": 626.0,
      "height": 33.75,
      "angle": 0,
      "strokeColor": "#17324d",

      "roundness": null,
      "seed": 1,
      "version": 1,
      "versionNonce": 1,
      "isDeleted": false,
      "boundElements": null,
      "updated": 1791527507119,
      "link": null,
      "locked": false,
      "text": "区间内部相邻两项都加 v，差值抵消；只留下两个边界。",
      "originalText": "区间内部相邻两项都加 v，差值抵消；只留下两个边界。",
      "fontSize": 25,
      "fontFamily": 2,
      "textAlign": "left",

    },
